A real public transit strike may constrain the fictional commute, but the system must know *where, when and how* it intersects that particular life. Public event admission is not the same as actor exposure, actor exposure is not the same as actor comprehension, and none automatically becomes an opinion prompt for Nadia. A user might correctly mention yesterday's news before Nadia has heard it; a relative may report an inaccurate account; an institution's actual policy may matter despite no one discussing it.

The same distinction holds inside the kitchen. The world may contain a utility bill whose cost and provenance are objective; the visitor might see only a folded page, while Nadia might falsely believe they read the amount. Their subsequent exchange can change material history without revealing any complete inner explanation. A truthful UX is not a series of prompt-driven canonical revelations: it is a historically particular **distribution of access, uncertainty and practical agency**. A seemingly inert object can remain inert forever.

The user arrives in a world already containing food, utility bills, schedules, buses, family routines, work, objects, strangers and a past they did not witness. What is in the room is not selected by what will make an engaging clue. A fridge magnet may be from an ordinary holiday; a pair of sandals may have been bought without any lasting emotional meaning; a photograph can have material provenance without a person presently remembering it. The event history is not interchangeable with anybody's account of it.

The original SOROK historical-bridge argument refuses two equally seductive failures. First, if a player notices an old photo, the system cannot retrospectively make that photo proof of any psychological theory the player proposed. Second, if the full past has not been generated in expensive detail, the world does not become historically empty until attention conjures it. Between these is constrained historical completion: current qualified world facts, already performed causes, admitted material consequences and bounded unresolved causal allocation delimit what may become determinate later. Some past details are harmless descriptions; others have episodic associations, genuine causal contributions or major developmental effects. Their grade cannot be decided merely by how narratively satisfying they sound.

An actor may possess several historically different kinds of “past”: a performed event, an ordinary recurring practice, an inferred compressed childhood period, a sensory trace, a remembered conversation, a belief about another's intention, a story told by a relative, and a myth about herself. These cannot be represented as interchangeable flashback text. A failed attempt to fix a toilet is objective history; Nadia's lasting view of the visitor's competence may remain uncertain. A relative's accusation can change family relations without changing who stole an object. A witnessed public catastrophe may affect housing and wages long before the actor learns its cause.

Historical openness must therefore become **more constrained as causally consequential facts accumulate**. An unresolved earlier slot does not give unlimited license to invent antecedents once a later scene needs them. At the same time historical closure cannot “repair” human contradictions out of existence. A person's remembered feelings may differ from an eyewitness report; a private story may survive contrary objective evidence; some causal contributions may remain genuinely underdetermined. Hard impossible chronologies belong to repair, while lawful inconsistency of human models belongs to the world.

Attention introduces a further difference between two frontiers. The world may acquire additional necessary material depth to remain causally sound; the visitor's epistemic frontier need not advance alongside it. Looking at the photo can yield more texture, readable text or evidence, yet cannot guarantee the user can identify the person, infer the event, know how Nadia regards it or access its future implications. Nadia may not herself know how the photograph came into her mother's possession. Her answer to a question is an independently situated act, perhaps truthful, mistaken, evasive or sincerely incomplete—not a database request returning the world's secret facts. **Actor testimony is an action, not a truth API**; the analytic system's explanatory authority does not become the person's first-person knowledge.

A strong account also refuses serendipity on demand. Ten objects ought not become ten symbols of Nadia's childhood. Some associations may be strong, some weak, some entirely absent; an old taste may be retained without ideological harmony; coincident circumstances may share a cause without having any “message.” The user may become better at recognizing actual recurring patterns while becoming less inclined to assume every detail belongs to one all-encompassing inner explanation. More knowledgeable does not imply more totalizing.

This is the historical form of the theory's central discipline: **there is one world, but many unfinished ways of knowing and inhabiting it**.

## 16. The world's constitution of Nadia is a condition of UX, not UX's jurisdiction

**The NEIGHBOR/CO boundary should sharpen, not weaken, the material psychology of the encounter.** NEIGHBOR does not independently decide the psychological structure through which Nadia will recruit a response; that belongs to SORACLE-CO's theory and its competent causal ownership. But NEIGHBOR must challenge that theory with scenes in which historically embodied life matters beyond propositional beliefs: wages paid or withheld, a parent's exhaustion, a teacher's institutional authority, race or gender as experienced under a particular society, a child learning a language, religious devotion that can sustain or discipline solidarity, and an intimate relation that can both enlarge agency and reproduce dependency. **No actor's attachment, values, motives or “health” are legible apart from the material relations and struggles through which they became possible.** The user encounters only a mediated fraction of this world, yet their own socially formed outlook is tested against it. Psychological depth is thus a condition of UX without the UX claiming ownership of psychological generation; the actual human's potential formation through those encounters *is* its own problem.

The question “Who is Nadia?” cannot be answered before asking into which historical relations she was born. Parentage, sexed embodiment, childhood dependency, land, property, wages, legal standing, cultural language, social class, migration, religion, the availability of care, and struggles beyond her household all participate in producing both objective possibilities and what a person learns those possibilities mean. A society is not a backdrop behind private psychology. Work can use or limit the body; institutions can enforce dependence; family obligations may reproduce unequal labor; an inherited language may make one feeling readily expressible while leaving another difficult to articulate.

But the inverse abstraction is also false: a person is not just an intersection of social categories. PYAT's actual respondent-level joint distributions and social/institutional constraints can ground a *possible life*, not infer her hidden psychology from ethnicity, religion or income. Social structure conditions likelihood and feasibility without prewriting personal meaning. Two people can occupy the same class and recognize different duties; someone can reproduce a practice they verbally reject; a minority configuration can be real even when the available empirical cell is small or uncertain. A sampled survey answer is an observation with wording, period, weights, selection and missingness, not proof of a fictional respondent's secret past. The whole-person adult entrance must preserve contradictory co-travel rather than flatten a respondent into independent averages.

There are two historical entrances into one continuing psychology. A PYAT-conditioned hypothetical adult can be constituted with a plausible, evidence-bounded compressed prehistory. Nadia, as an immature need-bearing organism, begins before the adult acquired forms are available: hungry, bodily vulnerable, developing orientation, sensorimotor ability, expectation and practical control through actual material interaction. Neither route creates a different metaphysical kind of person. A constructed adult may have a necessarily inferred past; a child may have a richly experienced developing one. Both must enter the same ongoing historical relations once constituted. An LLM can propose meanings, formations, causal patterns and new possibilities, but neither a survey nor a generative model has earned infallible access to why this particular person became herself.

The key **NEIGHBOR-UX** fusion is between the original SOROK social-field conception and the demand posed to the encounter by the Constitutive Organism's living psyche. UX does not specify the psyche's entire formation; it must understand what a historically formed person makes possible, refuses and transforms in an actual meeting. The world does not first produce fixed persons who then meet. Actual coactivity forms bodily capacities, anticipations, roles, inhibitions and routes of attention; those forms mediate later participation; participation changes the social field. A person grows through trying to act on an Other, meeting their independent response and encountering material success or obstruction. A child sings with her mother, begins to repeat a rhythm alone, later recognizes someone excluded from the singing, and may find in the learned good of shared participation a reason to challenge an authority who once made the practice possible. The acquired good can outgrow its source without making the source's earlier care unreal.

The same route can produce the reverse. The child can learn that care is protecting a fragile family order, and later demand obedience from a loved person precisely as an act of care. The world, the Other's independent need, the child's received experience and the objective harm must all remain distinguishable. Class, law and institutional power determine whether a moral insight can become an effective change. A person can become politically lucid while remaining materially trapped. Another can wield the language of liberation to reproduce domination.

Meeting a person from a different social world can have ethical force through **the distribution of unchosen historical contingencies**, without treating that person as a teaching exhibit. A visitor can see that what seemed universal in their own life depended on a contingent passport, household, language, gendered division of labor, war or lack of it. No narrator needs to declare that they learned the correct lesson. Another fictional person may occupy a far more privileged world position, or an apparently similar one with incompatible institutions. The prospect of worldwide distributions is not a moralizing set of country profiles; it is a possible form for encountering what was never chosen at birth. Neither one symbolic Nadia nor five richly developed seeds constitutes empirical planetary coverage.

Historically constituted personhood consequently does not demand that every action reveal class struggle, that every person possess an explicit theory of institutions, or that every object bear a dramatic meaning. A person's history contains labor, rest, private pleasure, repetitive boredom, incidental tastes, failed attempts, unavailable opportunities and opaque absences. Materialism is a commitment to actual causes and conditions, not a mandate for thematic exposition.

## 17. Care and coercion enter the encounter as real activities

**Care and social reproduction are not moral abstractions: they are hours, bodies, food, money, interruptions, fatigue and permissions distributed unequally among people.** The visit can disclose this without making a mother an exhausted-caregiver archetype or a child a sentimental lesson. A visitor offering help may free someone from one chore while imposing another, reinforce a gendered expectation, or sustain an already chosen shared practice; a refusal may protect a boundary or abandon a material obligation. The possibility of recognizing care as *labor* and labor as historically divided is part of Rawlsing's materialist and theological horizon. It can lead to sympathy, irritation, collective analysis, resistance, complicity or no explicit conversion. The ethical relation to a stranger emerges at the same point as structural power: who may assign work, who has no alternative, whose body bears the burden, and who receives credit or even notice.

Here NEIGHBOR-UX takes developmental theory as a **challenge to the conception of an encounter**, not as an original account of infant psychology. The infant's first responsiveness is not the discovery of an omnipotent personal force. A cry, movement, grasp or early attempt meets an independently acting caregiver whose labor and bodily conditions matter. Feeding, soothing, smiling, delay and refusal require time, food, work, power, other commitments and sometimes help from people the child has never seen. A sleeping child may have reorganized the night shifts of three adults without knowing it. A mother may be caring and exhausted; a partner may supply wages while undermining her freedom; another child may assist because they love their sibling or because they are compelled. The child's lived world is formed through relations among Others that exceed the child's own relationship with each.

This transforms “need satisfaction” from a private gauge into an encounter with material reproduction. The infant cannot yet conceive a good parent, equal care, a state policy or gendered labor. But actual bodily and relational history can form expectations of availability, initiative, waiting, caution, pleasure or resentment. Later reflection can take those acquired tendencies as objects: Nadia can resent her own dependence, feel shame at pleasure, fear her longing, or find relief in the permission to grieve. None of these configurations has to follow a universal ladder to self-conscious maturity. An adult's emotion can be a relation to another emotion or to their own relationship with an absent person, not an arithmetic blend of basic labels.

The original SOROK ethical charter and D007's “kenotic attention” become materially more exact here. Care cannot mean “cause the other to approve of me.” It is an attempt to answer to another's actual existence, which may include protecting their capacity to refuse me, their work, their body and their relationships beyond me. But the same language can conceal domination. A parent may genuinely believe harsh restriction is care, a pastor may recruit tenderness as a reason for humiliation, a sibling may impose surveillance in the name of protecting mother. Their sincerity does not make harm unreal. Conversely, refusal of an exploitative arrangement can be an act of care even when the recipient experiences it as betrayal.

Non-possession is therefore not noncommitment. Humans may choose marriage, exclusive relationships, mutual obligations, custody, dependence and promises. A life may legitimately become deeply organized around caring for a lover or child. What is forbidden as an explanatory collapse is to turn that commitment into ownership of the other person's entire future, or to confuse an institution's persistence with the living people it supposedly protects. This distinction leaves room for real love without a programmatic intimacy objective and real separation without a platform celebrating independence.

The visitor does not receive a score for recognizing these tensions. They enter a real-feeling social situation where another person's being costly, busy, wrong, vulnerable or unavailable can matter. The most generous act may receive no recognition; the most thoughtless one may be welcomed. Care, attachment, trust, health, pleasure, compliance, dependence, intimacy, benefit and access are separate historical relations, not nine names for an underlying goodness meter.

## 18. An acquired contradictory psyche confronts the human without being authored by UX

**For NEIGHBOR, the deepest psyche is not privileged hidden content waiting to be disclosed; it is a source of independently lived possibilities that no surface can exhaust.** The more historically contradictory Nadia becomes under SORACLE-CO's construction, the less legitimate it is for the interface to arrange revealing microexpressions, accidental confessions or therapeutic breakthroughs whenever the human attends well. She can sincerely act without knowing why, misremember an earlier event, revise a self-story, knowingly conceal, or respond in a way consistent with several incompatible explanations. The same concrete outward conduct can be significant and still unreadable. **Phenomenological non-exhaustion is not a lack of sufficient diagnostic clues.** It is the necessary space in which another person, historically formed and independently active, can encounter a human without being rendered as information for that human. The visitor may still become very competent at understanding specific tendencies and negotiating a particular relation.

The Constitutive Organism's crucial advance—developed under **SORACLE-CO's separate theoretical jurisdiction**—was to reject a list of fixed deep traits underneath a visible tip. NEIGHBOR-UX receives this as a requirement to conceive an encounter with an historically active, internally contradictory Other, not as a mandate to become the owner of her psyche. The person is a historically organized way of taking up a world. Bodily need, memory, affective anticipation, skill, culturally learned concepts, defenses, norms, pleasure, loyalties and partly inaccessible expectations jointly condition what becomes significant before the person consciously decides what they are feeling. The current tip—speech, action, refusal, silence, laughter—expresses only some of that organization.

The original PRE-A0 and R3 theory sharpened the difference between **genesis and eligibility**. A defensible response cannot merely say that APPROACH or WITHHOLD was eligible today; it must explain how the person's particular activity and relations made such an orientation possible, including what happened when earlier attempts succeeded, failed or were constrained. A first possible end may arise through the transformation of action into motive, repetition under altered conditions, imitation turned into an independent practice, or a sudden situated contrast. The possibility of a new undertaking can appear before competence has been proven; conversely, a technically combinable action may be unimaginable to the actor. Recognized desire, possible activity, attempt, material success and durable new capacity must not be collapsed.

But the deeper neglected shard is **psychological heterogeneity without purification**. Earlier SOROK warned that a person's “internally consistent” presentation may be too consistent to be alive. Nadia may value emancipation in a public organization while reproducing discipline at home; she may love her brother and oppose his restrictions; she may hold several moral frameworks with different reach. A contradiction can be explicit and agonizing, tacit and compartmentalized, or not consciously experienced as contradiction. Her own self-explanation can be coherent precisely because it excludes inconvenient evidence. The engine must not choose the best-fitting single motive at each action and then declare it to be the person.

An active disposition is not a contextless essence. A current situation can recruit a historically constituted configuration through a role, relation, analogous cue, memory, institution or bodily state. The same kindness can foster another person's independence in one setting and rationalize control in another. A historically formed inhibition can be directly active, indirectly influential, dormant, attenuated, transformed or genuinely no longer functional. There is no conserved universal scalar of emotional energy. “No durable change” is as legitimate a history as a transformation. A brief intense meeting can leave nothing; a year of ordinary work can alter what Nadia can bear or do without producing a narratable revelation.

Here is what the fusion forces: **the more precisely we locate a contradiction's center of gravity, the more concrete and internally multiple the person becomes**. Discovering that love, fear, obligation, resistance and pleasure can arise through one history of care does not select an ultimate motive. It discloses additional determinate tensions: gratitude against refusal, competence against obedience, solidarity against dependence, remembered joy against present repudiation, and differences among the recipients of the same act. Each tension opens still further historical questions. Some remain dormant, some erupt, some alter their relation, some acquire new forms; none has to disappear to make the theory intelligible. Theoretical maturation is the *capacity to derive more living differences through their relations*, including contradictions Nadia cannot presently formulate. Her partial knowledge of herself is not a defect the HOME is trying to cure.

## 19. Objects, practices and institutions carry life into the room

**The visitor meets not just private individuals but human activity deposited in objects, labor divisions, customs and institutions.** A household appliance condenses wages, supply chains, skill, convenience and gendered work; a borrowed tool can carry trust, resentment or sheer practicality; a religious image may be prayer, kinship inheritance, political boundary, decoration or all of these at different moments. An object does not automatically reveal its social meaning on inspection. Nor does the fact that human capacities are objectified mean they are automatically alienated. **Objectification is ordinary historical life; alienation and commodity fetishism arise in determinate relations of production and power**, not because an interpretive model assigns an object a high symbolic score. The UX can let a visitor actually encounter the price, repair, labor, ownership or obligation through which material things mediate their relation, and can permit a religious language of vocation or neighbor-love to live alongside—not substitute for—these concrete questions.

**The UX encounter is with human powers already objectified in the material world:** labor in the meal and house, care in a repaired object, power in a credential or wage demand, and inherited practical meanings in an institution. The visitor does not receive the history as explanatory text; they meet its present constraints and affordances through actual participation. An object may be inert, meaningful, oppressive or liberating in different relations without the interface assigning it a universal symbolic function.

An everyday thing is never only a narrative prop, yet it need not carry secret destiny. An unpaid bill is a claim on future labor and liquidity as well as paper on a table. A repaired shoe may embody skill, scarcity, wear, care or habit. A wedding ring can carry a mutual promise, a coercive institution, a private memory or all three. A child's song can bear the labor of the mother who taught it while becoming something the child later practices without her. An inherited family home can protect people and materially restrict their ability to leave.

The original 53–56 value/objects work and the Constitutive Organism R3 identify a deeper possibility: **human activity objectifies capacities and relations into things, practices and institutions that subsequently shape what people can do and value**. Objectification is not itself an error. A built house, practiced song, language or shared ritual can enlarge human life. The contradiction emerges when an objectified relation is reified into an authority that overrides the living relation from which its legitimacy arose. THE MARRIAGE can demand the sacrifice of partners; the GOOD SON can demand a sister's obedience; THE CHURCH can demand victims' silence; THE REVOLUTION can be invoked to dominate comrades. The institution's actual property rights, reputation, resources and sanctions can make these claims materially effective even if they are false or oppressive.

This is not an excuse to treat capitalist commodity fetishism as a universal metaphor for every touching keepsake. Capital's labor and exchange relations have historical specificity. Nor does a religious object authorize the simulation to pronounce on God's existence. The same devotional sign may organize solidarity, grief, rebellion, hierarchy or contradiction according to actual actors and institutions. The world contains material coercion and independent human interpretation; neither is a single function of the other.

An important fusion with NEIGHBOR's own design appears here. The platform also **objectifies** a possible person into a shareable root and an encounter product. The user may cherish Nadia, but the service must not then make that constructed relationship into a commodity that issues claims on the user's life—compulsory check-ins, emergency purchases, romantic retention pressure, payment for more agreeable behavior. A simulation of nonpossessive encounter can itself become a machine of possession if it treats user attention as an extractable resource.

There are two different authorities to question: the fictional institution that tells Nadia how to live, and the actual platform that controls what the visitor can encounter. Neither should disappear beneath the other. The richer NEIGHBOR theory must make this symmetry visible without turning every scenario into a sermon against institutions.

## 20. The person rereads herself; the visitor never owns the interpretation

**The right to interpret is distributed among persons, and interpretation itself can become a new cause of history.** Nadia may re-narrate a repeated family practice after discovering betrayal, not by altering the original act but by finding a different significance in it; the visitor may see a liberatory gesture where Nadia sees humiliating charity, or regard a sincere confession as an attempt at manipulation. Later behavior may derive from either misrecognition, mutual argument, a decision not to interpret or a genuinely revised practical aim. The interface should not appoint a final analyst to explain which view was “really” the psychological truth. **Truth of occurrence, source of testimony, causal contribution, subjective reorganization and normatively defensible judgment are different registers.** Their contradiction is precisely why interpretation can transform persons and relations without becoming sovereign over the past.

The original SOROK 71–73 psychological theory, whose maturation belongs with SORACLE-CO, imposes perhaps the strongest encounter-theory challenge on the current HOME: meaning has a history distinct from occurrence, and an interpretation can become a route through which later reality is received. An actual event occurred; it had material contributors, some perhaps uncertain. A later recurrence may share a cause or only a resemblance. Nadia may notice the resemblance, assign significance, weave it into a self-story, ask another person, revise it jointly, or reject it. These are distinct levels. When the interpretation begins to alter action, it becomes a new cause without becoming the event's original cause.

At twenty-three Nadia may remember a period as liberation, excitement and discovering her real self. At twenty-nine she may regard it as vanity, credulity or dangerous self-deception. At thirty-five she may become kinder to both prior selves, finding truth in the opening of the earlier period and real warning in the later critique. These are not three permanent belief versions overwritten by the newest, nor a mandatory dialectic progressing toward final synthesis. Each self-interpretation becomes material for later interpretation. The age-thirty-five synthesis can exclude or mythologize other facts. More generosity toward past selves does not prove full reality contact.

An interpretation can become **routing**. If Nadia acquires a framework in which all disagreement proves she is surrounded by enemies, another person's reasonable objection may strengthen the framework instead of challenging it. She can achieve greater subjective coherence while becoming less responsive to actual counterevidence. A liberationist framework can correctly expose domination, help her act and preserve her life, and also overextend, brutally reinterpret ambiguous memories or turn into a newly exclusive authority. The opposite is possible: discomfort with a cherished institution can fracture a confident self-story and make room for practices she once could not conceive. The change has no predetermined moral sign. It may be generous, defensive, tyrannical, necessary, confused or unresolved.

The central distinction is **reality pressure versus correct interpretation**. A missed opportunity, another person's resistant testimony, bodily failure, material debt or a memory that does not fit places pressure upon the present framework. The actor may accommodate, appropriate, partition, ignore, externalize, destabilize, transform or hold the contradiction unresolved. These are different historical possibilities, not a fixed response ladder or new universal operator store. A framework's scope, practical binding, accessibility of counterevidence and dependence upon institutions can matter without becoming one “integration” score.

This also changes what the visitor's knowledge can do. A visitor may point out a pattern, offer a new story, ask an honest question or supply information that changes Nadia's understanding. Their theory of her may be wrong and still acquire real influence through her response. They may offer a truth she rejects, or a falsehood she makes part of herself. The game cannot reward user interpretation with automatic confirmation. Nor can the narrator “reveal Nadia's true childhood meaning” merely because a clever player notices repeated symbols.

**The greater NEIGHBOR-UX's generative power, the more sharply it must make Nadia's historically generated, proliferating contradictions **encounterable without artificially manufacturing or resolving them**.** A changed self-myth is not a vote to delete one prior self. It places earlier self-relations into a new relation, creating further pressures among their still-effective histories. The theory's work is not to place Nadia on a journey toward a clean final integration, but to discern the historical germ through which one reinterpretation discloses new tensions in desire, memory, work, the Other's resistance and the values she has inherited. Her self-unity, where she experiences one, is itself a changing and contestable historical formation.


## 21. The generative kernel is the increasingly capable encounter

**The complete encounter is the historical development of two asymmetrically constituted participants through a world neither controls, mediated by a platform whose own power must be kept answerable.** That is the new, more exact center of gravity. Nadia was produced through material and psychological history that precedes the visitor; her changing orientations and acts are SORACLE-CO's domain. The actual human comes with a different unchosen history, capacities and dispositions. NEIGHBOR's power is to let that human *actually exercise* attention, speech, judgment, social tact, memory, practical cooperation, resistance and departure under lawful conditions where the Other can receive, misinterpret, ignore, oppose or be changed by the encounter. Independent material events and institutions mediate what both can do. The platform then chooses what part of the resulting world the visitor can lawfully experience. **Every one of those mediations is historically productive, and every one can be mistaken for sovereignty over the others.**

This makes the original fragments generate one another. Material class homology without biographical equivalence exposes the formation of judgment and habitus. The meaningfully performed human operation versus local bodily chance makes competence a social relation, not a stat. Actor reception independent from user-lens narration makes the Other unowned by the interface. A lens that becomes more faithful to the visitor's habits can become more ideologically enclosing, thereby generating a reason for reflexive awareness of perception itself. Repeated social practice creates reliable local competence while admitting that the two participants attach different meanings to it. Access is formed by actual relations, law, property, obligations and permissions, not by a relationship level. Plural flourishing means an affectionate relation can be coercive and a rejected act can be caring; it cannot be rescued by an invisible scalar. A public gesture or utterance makes historical commitments, while actor testimony, human interpretation and later self-narration remain capable of falsehood. Intimacy and rupture return to household labor and the plurality of lives, rather than becoming the consumable climax of someone's existence.

**The philosophical and theological motive now resides in those contradictions themselves.** Neighbor-love names an ethical orientation toward a person one does not own; kenotic attention relinquishes claims to exhaustive representation, not the capacity to oppose injustice; solidarity is a contingent historical practice involving actual material costs, not a badge for having felt empathy; critique of ideology begins with the recognition that what seems obvious has a social history, including when that obviousness is digitally optimized by the platform. Every demand has a material translation and can be disputed: who works, who pays, who knows, who is allowed to refuse, what the platform hides, which institutions sustain freedom, and whose dependency has become coercion. The theology is not an algorithm that silently selects happy endings, and a simulated actor's modeled viewpoint is not proof of consciousness or theological personhood. It is the **actual human's encounter and possible formation** that are unambiguously real social stakes here.

**The kernel increases NEIGHBOR's explanatory organization without purifying Nadia or the visitor of contradiction.** A user may gain genuine practical and epistemic power, become more accountable or more manipulative, learn a model that works locally but fails elsewhere, correct an earlier wrong judgment yet lose a relationship, or continue to misunderstand someone they love. Increased relational specificity can coexist with separation, better prediction with a narrower lens, genuine care with complicity, actual success with moral harm, forgiveness with renewed vulnerability, and no visible response with profound actor-private activity. None is mandatory content; each becomes lawful under the causal field that produces it. *Non-exhaustion* does not mean artificially blocking a good interpretation, and *resistance* does not mean manufacturing antagonism. Rawlsing must allow accurate knowing and effective action when warranted; the future stays open because independent people and institutions continue beyond that locally valid knowledge.

Finally, the original Astra strata provide the methodological check on the whole: **A** moment; **B** scene; **C** day; **D** recurrent continuity; **E** full one-person history including departure; **F** globally dispersed other lives; **G** actual-human shared roots/invitations; **H** the user's longer history with Rawlsing. These are possible experiential scales, not eight progression levels or a single hidden game loop. Each must be derived from its own phenomena. The minute must have possible value without a promise of future revelation; a solo person-continuity must stand without social comparison; public discourse must not convert fictional lives into answers to a global moral quiz; user reflection must remain voluntary and non-diagnostic. How engaging these forms ultimately prove to be remains open to empirical study. **The product does not become a game because we declare a loop. It may become playable when the human's genuine possibilities encounter independent actuality and irreversible, still-open history.**

**Why this is RAWLSING UX and not merely a sophisticated story interface:** The project does not aim to make the human *feel* more virtuous after a scene. It seeks to stage a participation in which the material production of persons becomes encounterable and the visitor may become differently organized through **actual decisions under irreducible plurality**. The real does not appear as a preannounced moral; it resists, exceeds and sometimes corrects the visitor's theory of it. The visitor's previous habits—how they allocate scarce attention, present themselves, respond to boundaries, distribute help, seek validation, revise interpretations, tolerate ambiguity, accept dependence, or withdraw—may be exercised and transformed in these interactions. The game has not achieved its purpose merely because these behaviors were observable, nor has it failed because the human did not experience a moment of revelation. **User-formation is a historically open possibility, not a product-authored verdict.**

This unifies rather than adds two opposed truths: the **philosophical/theological horizon** names the significance of meeting an Other who is not mine to finish, and the **particular mechanics** refuse to let that significance be accomplished through declaration alone. Class transposition gives material contingency a price; finite attention makes situated knowledge costly; interrupted action makes intention encounter an actual world; independent reception makes love and misrecognition two possible afterlives of one act; relationship history makes practical obligations possible; ordinary time deprives the visitor of a savior narrative; the right to leave resists platform appropriation. These forms can generate the very contradictions through which an actual human thinks, desires and changes. Yet none guarantees moral conversion, psychological integration, divine disclosure or social liberation. **A theory that can explain why the experience might form the human, and why it might instead intensify domination, has more life than either a mechanical specification or a predetermined ethical pilgrimage.**

**The deepest tension across UX and the compressed organism is now visible.** At one scale, the synthetic history must use selected events dense enough to stand for much larger temporal and social processes. At another, the encounter must permit a moment in which no new explanation, emotion or lasting psychic change occurs. There is no contradiction to eliminate by forcing every live turn to matter: **historical selection and interpersonal availability are different grains of reality**. A representative developmental episode can be chosen because it clarifies a recurrent relation, yet the visitor's lunchtime remark within that episode can be ignored, forgotten or misunderstood. The relation between grains—not a guarantee of significance for every human prompt—is what permits a deep world to remain ordinary and open-ended.

This transforms the kernel from a rule about an interface into a theory of **situated exposure to condensed yet inexhaustible historical life**. The human is given enough material for real choice, never enough to own the scene; the scene is historically representative without being a destiny machine; the person can be consequential without becoming a protagonist in every event. Finding this axis lets UX become more organizationally capable: it can represent crowded social time, scarcity of attention, different epistemic channels and partial causal effects in fewer explicit scenes, *while their experienced possibilities become more differentiated*. Compression should increase the power to represent the manifold without reducing that manifold to a narrative outline.

**The kernel's motion is not “the world gets more content when the human does more.”** Each specific expansion in practical access may multiply the human's possibilities while revealing new constraints. Seeing a document may disclose the financial charge but not who feels burdened by it; becoming a trusted cook may create a standing routine and expose new friction over food, labor, time or who has final authority in the kitchen; hearing a person's story may make the relationship deeper while revealing that both previously understood a shared convention differently. A correct observation may generate a mistaken response; a true interpretation may cause change without becoming a universal explanation.

The late SOROK audits gave a particularly powerful possible evaluation signature for UX: the visitor's **local model fidelity can increase while their claimed totalization decreases**. They may make increasingly accurate situated predictions, remember historical particulars and recognize social boundaries more precisely while becoming less confident that they can compress Nadia into a label or final diagnosis. This is an *unverified research hypothesis*, not a moral scoreboard or product progress statistic. The principle belongs in the theory because it describes how a maturing encounter can **increase usable knowledge and awareness of excess simultaneously**. That is not contradiction to be eliminated; it is an axis of life.

A further negative control distinguishes the user genuinely changing someone's trajectory from a computational savior arc. A visitor may articulate an idea that Nadia later acts on; the eventual consequence may matter immensely. But neither the previous history nor the character's current needs may be rearranged so the visitor was necessarily “the missing key.” Their action is one possible new cause among other conditions; its actual effect may be partial, opposed, delayed, or imperceptible to them. The difference between *a world that can be changed through participation* and *a world arranged to confirm the visitor's centrality* is the generative discipline of NEIGHBOR-UX.

**Now the deepest UX fusion can be named.** The separate SOROK shards once asked about the veil, player identity, first contact, embodiment, task granularity, relationship progression, attention, time, messaging, other actors, economic cost, invitations, privacy and noncommodification. Each looked like a different product problem. Together they reveal a **generative nucleus of the encounter**: *another person's life creates a field of possible participation; the real human enters through a limited aperture, performs actual acts under situated constraints, receives only part of the results, and is thereafter able to participate differently because that shared history happened*. This does not tell SORACLE-CO how Nadia's motives are made. It tells UX how **the history of a relation changes the structure of access, attention, action, expectation and consequence**.

The old pieces multiply under that nucleus. The visitor's exact initial class homology turns ordinary spending into a lived question without granting financial mind-reading. The task-entry rule turns a goal sentence into an invitation to *perform* capability, not just specify it. A learned domestic routine makes subsequent compression more natural because familiarity is historically real; this increases efficiency while increasing the specificity of what can still interrupt the routine. A phone number changes the ecology of relation without creating perpetual availability. A natural termination makes absence meaningful without licensing guilt optimization. A true social introduction can invite a new real human or open a common fictional origin without creating property in another person. The same source-root supports divergent encounters because the human act is constitutive of branch history but never author of all worlds.

**This kernel increases representational organization without diminishing the contradictions of experience.** The better UX differentiates social routes, the more possibilities it can represent for simultaneous welcome and boundary, intimacy and privacy, goodwill and harmful effect, action and nonresponse, equivalent cost and unshared knowledge, meaningful speech and failed reception, continuity and irrevocable departure. The more differentiated the aperture, the more alive and unfinishable the encounter becomes—not because UX manufactures a more elaborate psyche but because it can encounter and participate in more of what the independently constituted person and world make possible.

The old D007 kernel search exposes a mistake that can infect NEIGHBOR-UX theory-MULCH itself. **Its sought kernel concerns the form, contradictions and possibilities of an encounter with an independent life.** It is not a substitute for the separate psychological kernel SORACLE-CO is working to discover. The wish for one powerful formula may force false compression. But it would be the opposite error to abandon the search for **the axes, germ, seed, spine or center of gravity of the contradictions**. The point of discovering such a center is not to explain away opposed forms. It is to see **how they generate and continually complicate one another**, so the same germ can produce many historically specific outcomes, some mutually antagonistic. An elegant relation that cannot produce new and recalcitrant differences is a false kernel. The true depth of the HOME must be judged by how many previously disconnected phenomena it can *differentiate through their material relations*, not by how much of their distinct reality it compresses into a name. The unity may have several irreducible poles, and an actual person may inhabit incompatible organizations with no final synthesis. “Single HOME” means all must be articulated as one living conception, **not that every aspect of life reduces to one axiom**.

The currently visible **generative axes of contradiction** have real derivational power. Their purpose is to multiply concrete possibilities and expose new conflicts as they mediate one another:

**Historical and material constitution:** persons and possibilities arise through bodies, institutions, labor and actual relation rather than a fixed trait catalogue.

**Plural, non-sovereign centers:** each person's perception, valuation and activity has a particular standpoint, while no person has full jurisdiction over another's experience, over the world or even over their own unrecognized history.

**Constrained non-exhaustion:** objective admitted facts endure, but a person, relation and world are not identical to any one presentation, model or personal theory of them.

**Real reciprocal causation:** encounters and independently caused events can change material conditions, relations and future psychic recruitment, including through false interpretations, failure, absence and ordinary repetition.

**Non-teleological personhood and human agency:** any participant can want, care, injure, leave, commit, learn, refuse, become dependent or remain indifferent without an externally mandated improvement arc.

These jointly explain why the user cannot author Nadia's world by asking, why ATTEND discovers rather than manufactures, why the narrator lacks the authority of a confession, why relations can change a person without becoming her total identity, why a shared seed can diverge after contact, why a benefit can occur without recognition, and why a later self-myth does not erase earlier objective history.

But they do not necessitate the precise product design. **Exactly homologous SES at entry**, a neighbor across the landing, the particular resources bar, one/three/five alpha people, a scarce Roll, human invitation policy, the choice to offer messaging, and the presentation of a first-contact prelude are authored decisions. They are fruitful because they render parts of the underlying conception experientially available while remaining contingent and revisable. We should be able to explain *what each choice accomplishes* without proclaiming it a law of human psychology.

A completed HOME therefore has three intertwined registers, not three separate documents: (a) the historically constitutive conception of person-world-relation; (b) the voluntarily constructed experience through which a real human meets another particular life; (c) the independent consequences, limits and institutional obligations introduced by hosting that experience. The design choices can strengthen one another: matched SES makes material cost felt without other-person account disclosure; a shared root makes branch-specific becoming socially discussable; a thin portal lets an actual human bring an unsimulated mind; the two encounter windows make access itself a historical relation; the resource bar contextualizes an act without explaining its meaning.

And precisely where these choices collide with the deeper conception, they must be corrected. A “Nadia-centered” interface becomes corrupt if it makes the world a Nadia-only stage. A shared root becomes corrupt if it imports every user's history into one collective personality. A lovingly detailed iceberg becomes corrupt if every person is forced to possess one true explanation of every action. A strong moral charter becomes corrupt if it compels real users to remain, forgive or pursue intimacy. A coherent theory becomes corrupt if it erases the lawful contradictions by which a particular life becomes what it is.

## 22. A human at the threshold of several independently becoming lives

**Return to one ordinary evening and let all these shards become mutually necessary.** The visitor crosses a threshold into a class-homologous neighborhood and sees Nadia carrying groceries. A few feet away she has a folded bill near the refrigerator. The visitor may recognize a practical burden but cannot read the amount from across the room; their lens may bring the paper to notice because they frequently attend to household accounts, or may instead notice a child's drawing. Neither presentation changes the bill. They say, “I can take the heavy bags,” and turn toward the counter. The help enters a real practical situation: the steps, groceries, Nadia's actual burden, a child's interruption, time before work. They cannot complete the task by declaring themselves helpful. Their hands are occupied when Nadia tosses keys; a lucky catch may impress her, a miss may irritate her, and neither certifies the real human's reflexes.

The visitor speaks of a literary figure; Nadia laughs and infers they know the work well. In fact the user may know only the reference's social function. Rawlsing can have evidence of demonstrated speech competence without licensing a claim about external education or giving Nadia a secret internal report. As the visitor reaches for the bags, Nadia glances toward the hallway. If the surface reports the glance as an actual visible event, it becomes part of what happened; yet it does not prove she was anxious about the bill or noticed the visitor's glance. Later Nadia says she already paid the bill. That utterance is historical even if the proposition is false. The visitor may learn the truth later, or never. One observation becomes a cause of suspicion; another affects nothing. The child may remember the joke rather than the help.

A later conversation creates a routine that the visitor experiences as intimate and Nadia experiences as ordinary neighborly coordination. The platform must not adjudicate which is the hidden “relationship truth.” They may negotiate the meaning, misunderstand each other for months, enjoy the ritual or stop it. If a real affection develops, she might willingly reorder her schedule; the system must not make her scarce merely to preserve “independence.” Conversely an intense argument may leave the visitor with reduced access while Nadia's family, job, prayer, unpaid bills and exhausted body continue to require attention. A third party may know something neither understands. If a friend elsewhere played a parallel shared-root Nadia, their external testimony can inform the real visitor without magically becoming their in-world character's first-hand knowledge.

**What has changed is not the user's percentage completion of Nadia.** They have participated in a practical history containing gains, false attributions, unequally distributed knowledge, permissions, ordinary labor, possibly domination, and an open future. Their own habits of noticing, offering, bluffing, interpreting and refusing may have been exercised and perhaps altered. Or they may have merely enjoyed a meal. Nadia's psychological life is neither exposed to the narrator nor generated by the visitor's demand that it be interesting. This is why NEIGHBOR is not simply a portal with an NPC and not simply a theology lecture dressed as a kitchen. It gives the actual human *a limited but consequential place in someone else's lived historical field*.

**The experience in one uninterrupted line:** A real human accepts a truthful invitation and receives a restrained challenge to meet a particular life. They cross a perceptible portal threshold into a situated peer body, with Nadia's exact initial socioeconomic burden but not her history or bank balance. Someone is already doing something. The visitor looks, asks, acts, discovers material affordances, chooses when the medium can actually carry their thought, and delegates the routine where it cannot or need not. The world keeps time. The Other may notice, misread, invite, ignore, disagree or leave; her child and coworkers may act differently still. The user can come to know something while also becoming differently known. At a natural scene ending, they return to a different interface; a messaging route exists only if somebody made it possible. A later reply, missed invitation, chance meeting or ordinary routine gives the next contact a history that did not exist before. The game may have become deeply affecting or not. **The next possible participation has changed because an actual relation now has a past**.

That is where NEIGHBOR-UX can become theoretically inexhaustible. Its progress is **not collecting the rest of Nadia**, but learning and extending the lawful means through which one irreducible participant can engage another's already ongoing, material, contradictory life. SORACLE-CO's theory of psychological becoming is the richest neighbor to this conception. Its future deliberate fusion with UX must make both deeper, but no present theoretical round may quietly appropriate its jurisdiction.

A source-qualified possible person is constituted in historical and material conditions nobody selected for her. Her body changes; needs are met, frustrated and transformed; practices are acquired through Others; social institutions distribute work, resources, power and opportunity. Her family and wider world contain independent people related to one another. She develops expectations, defenses, loves, projects, habits, skills, pleasures, and partially inaccessible organizations of meaning. Some of these relate harmoniously; others contradict or exclude one another without requiring resolution.

The history becomes operative when present conditions recruit parts of that organization. A bodily sensation, an ongoing practical project, an institutional demand, a remembered song, another person's gesture, an old self-story or a material object can shape what is noticed, imagined, attempted or refused. That present activity has actual physical and social results; it does not automatically match motive or interpretation. Several observers receive different aspects of it, may misrecognize one another, and may subsequently act on those misrecognitions. The objective event remains historically true even when stories about it change. A new practice, personal expectation or interpretation may form, persist unchanged, weaken, become defensive or not take root at all. Ordinary time continues through repetition and changing world conditions.

**What follows is NEIGHBOR-UX's conception of the playable encounter; the preceding historical account of Nadia's psyche is a constitutive condition supplied and disputed in reciprocal theory with SORACLE-CO, not UX's authority to write her inner genesis.** The actual human opens a portal to meet this person. Their visitor position is initially economically homologous to hers, but their real mind is not replaced with a generated autobiography. A bounded fictional prelude is admissible because entering the game authorizes the social setup; Nadia's life supplies the actual plausible occasion for it. The human can choose how to speak, attend and act through the local embodied and material constraints, but cannot choose what Nadia thinks, what the world contains or the success of an intended intervention. The portal reveals a thin lawful phenomenal surface; the house contains more than the visitor sees; a messaging channel may become available through mutual history; absence does not abolish the person's ongoing life.

The visitor's own economic choices have contextual cost, perhaps visible through a brief resources-bar blink. The bar does not reveal Nadia's account, and their initially matched finances may later diverge unnoticed. A meal can become kindness, discomfort, debt, routine, a third-party conflict or nothing special. A genuine project can form, or a relationship can end. Another person may change through the visitor's influence while Nadia barely changes; Nadia may change where the visitor never looks. The visitor can become more careful, less certain, more possessive or no different. Nothing in the architecture optimizes either intimacy or independence.

Many real humans may meet a common historically sourced Nadia-root along separately developing branches. Their real discussion of divergent encounters can alter human knowledge without blending branch histories. New fictional origins may eventually be commissioned through a scarce Roll while remaining outside the ownership of those who initiated them. A platform can host all this only by maintaining real-world privacy, research, funding and distribution boundaries against the temptation to turn generated lives and real attention into commodities.

**What is the game?** The actual human wants to meet someone. To be with them, to know them, to talk, reason, joke, help, argue, love, refuse, revisit, misunderstand, sometimes hurt or be hurt. They enter a world in which their acts matter but are not sovereign; in which truth exists beyond their knowledge; in which another person's history may become partly their shared history; in which the wish to know can never become permission to own.

**What is the person?** Not the output of a personality type, a completed explanatory dossier, an observed dyad or a list of dramatic scenes. A historically organized, embodied fictional life with changing capacities to become: a life whose past constrains but does not fully predetermine its future; whose private organization and public manifestations do not coincide; whose relations matter without exhausting her; whose current meanings may be correct, mistaken or unresolved; whose becoming continues whether or not the visitor appears.

**What does the fusion make newly visible?** Each relation becomes a **germ of further contradictions**, not a reconciled pair: the same non-exhaustion that makes the Other unknowable through a single scene also makes further knowledge possible, which changes the Other's future conduct and produces new unknowability; historically formed care can create solidarity and legitimate domination, then furnish the cared-for person with a reason for refusal, which can itself be recruited as a form of control; the same initial material equivalence that makes a purchase cost mutually intelligible can conceal later difference, produce generosity or humiliation, and reorganize what either person believes about the other; a factually mistaken interpretation can create an actual consequence that changes what subsequently counts as available evidence; a shared person-root can make social comparison possible while separately acquired branch histories make the very phrase “same Nadia” dialectically unstable; the platform that opens access to another life can convert the user's attention into commodified dependence unless its own social powers are confronted. None of these contradictions needs to be harmonized or declared equally benign. Their **developing centers of gravity** are what make the HOME increasingly fertile. Their multiplication is the growth of the conception's concrete life.

The living circuit may be stated without claiming one final universal algorithm:

**Unchosen material and social life → embodied dependency and practical formation → historically acquired psychic relations and capacities → current activity among independent Others and institutions → situated human participation → one actual world occurrence and several unequal receptions → actor-specific interpretation, memory and action → material, relational and psychic afterlife (including non-change) → new conditions for possible life.**

The circuit has no computationally chosen happy ending. It is not a theory of guaranteed human goodness. It is the form within which affection, tragedy, ordinary pleasure, conflict, inquiry, domination, repair and transformation can arise in their particular lives.

**There is a game because the visitor wants to know someone. There is a world because that someone does not exist to be known.**

And from their encounter, new possibilities can arise that neither the visitor nor that someone possessed beforehand.

## 23. Source-conservation, explicit alternatives, and what this HOME still does not settle

**Round eleven / exact source-recovery correction (November 21):** The reconstituted active theory draws newly against the preserved original SOROK sources at historical commit \`865b5b01e1a27e99020cf2bb389af8cd4c874b57\`: D008 adaptive-lens product theory and its separate D007 research kernel candidate; D008 user-model / signaling noncollapse; D009 five human-faculty/capability authority classes and optional physical-chance modalities; D003 open flourishing court plus Court 10; SOROK 62 ordinary-life re-entry and noncommodification; 58 Q-PRIV purpose-limited user dossier/deletion; 69 cross-user source-root knowledge/retro-resolved precontact truth; 100 minimal-white-whale alpha's irreducible causal floor; 101 the exact binding outward event and ontic/phenomenal distinction; the 96 October 7 post-course-correct harvest, and Astra's native-gameplay, eight-strata, negative-court and failed-game-loop evidence. These were not imported as an autonomous newer authority or copied as doctrines. They sharpen the inner relation of the existing 23 movements: §§1–6, 8–15, 16–22 are reworked to make UX a generative field of *user-formation through mediated encounters with historically formed Otherness*. The exact origin, provisional or open grades and distinct CORE/UX owner boundaries are preserved in the existing cumulative source ledger. **A source named here is source custody and authorial conceptual use, not independent scientific endorsement, full semantic proof or executed joined code.**

**Newly exposed open theoretical questions:** (a) where does helpful user-lens customization cross into self-confirming ideology and institutional capture, especially with optional lens perturbation and profiling disabled? (b) how can genuine competence, skillful signaling, portaled bodily capacity, disability, device confounding and actor misattribution coexist without an achievement economy? (c) how can the published exact surface remain binding without forbidding actor falsehood or human partial hearing? (d) how can plural flourishing make objective harm and oppression thinkable without a universal cultural norm or hidden moral meter? (e) how can intense desire, dependence, refusal, sex, injury or rupture remain lawful possibilities without content commodification or engineered distance? (f) where is the moral and aesthetic significance of the encounter if many users find an ordinary life boring, and why would the platform be entitled to manufacture drama to compensate? (g) what does user self-formation mean when gaining better local knowledge may also deepen enclosure? (h) how should genuine external-human meta-knowledge from a shared root remain useful without branch-world omniscience or privacy breach? These are productive contradictions, not a list of new modules awaiting implementation.

**User interruption and controlling theoretical correction in this UX round:** The original theoretical/philosophical/psychological/theological premises are **not background motives to be summarized once before the UX mechanisms begin**. They are the generative reasons those encounter forms were chosen. The original \`01_PRODUCT_CHARTER.md\`, Court 10 \`24_COURT10_FLOURISHING_MORAL_PHYSICS_R1.md\`, Court 11 \`25_COURT11_PLAYER_PRAXIS_REFLECTION_R1.md\`, original D002 interaction constitution's **“Spiritual implication”**, and D007's hostile kernel tests (all read from source cut \`a4211c3ec77e8269097601fe9a3dce2745395ac0\`) jointly demand the present rearticulation: contingency as material position rather than a costume; user-formation through praxis instead of empathy grading; plural flourishing without one scalar; neighbor-love and kenotic attention without self-erasure or compulsory intimacy; image-bearing language as theological horizon rather than proof of machine consciousness; and normative critique translatable into institutions, material agency and consequences. These were not added as a detachable moral appendix: the opening germ and movements 3–5, 8, 10, 12, 21 have been rewritten to make each major UX operation carry its constitutive reason. The live user explicitly corrected the threat of losing this “juice” while recovering mechanics. Psychological constitution still belongs to SORACLE-CO; **the actual user's possible formation through the encounter** is a NEIGHBOR-UX responsibility.

**Newest user-directed and reciprocal UX source recovery:** \`SOROK_PRODUCT_CONTROL_ROOM/USER_DIRECTED_ARCHITECTURE/20261106_USER_SETTLED_SPARSE_EVENT_HYPERCOMPRESSION_AND_OPAQUE_OTHER_R1.md\` (2026-11-06, **user-directed product intent**, not native scientific authorization) requires sparse representative episodes, an art/science distinction, historical density without theatrical trauma, opacity and no invented perfect-life reconstruction. The UX encounter's own consequences are now reconstituted in movements 6, 12 and 21. \`SOROK_PRODUCT_CONTROL_ROOM/PROGRAMMA_SOROK/29_NADIA_SENSORY_ACCESS_FROM_PLAYER_CONDUCT_CANDIDATE_20261109_R1.md\` (2026-11-09, **user-supplied candidate requirement**, neither executed proof nor adoption) distinguishes actual outward visitor conduct from lawful actor sensory access and subsequent actor interpretation. Its UX implication now informs movement 8 and the existing chapters on ATTEND, reciprocal suspicion and no mind-reading. These direct source statuses have not been inflated into runtime authority or a merger with SORACLE-CO.

**Further original UX shards recovered in this pass:** the D002 core docket's original **eleven** subordinate theory artifacts (interaction, attention-time, mutual observation, non-curatorial detail, fifty scenario probes, player embodiment, epistemic assertion, concurrent action, stable world causality, actor-specific reception and delayed effects) at historical SOROK source cut \`a4211c3ec77e8269097601fe9a3dce2745395ac0\` supply the specific countercases now integrated into movements 4–5, 8, 12, 14–15 and 21. Particularly important: SCE-025 no bespoke reaction; SCE-033 user false credit; SCE-035 multi-actor divergent uptake; SCE-037 hidden activation with no tell; SCE-038 understood but not answered; SCE-039 valid-empty; SCE-040 speech preceding self-recognition; SCE-041 no renderer psychological rescue. Historical statuses remain provisional; these scenarios are adversarial source cases, not claims that a runtime passed them.

The old SOROK R12 stronger-version audits \`78_POST_FIFTH_BANKING_TAIL_THIRD_PASS_RESIDUAL_AUDIT_R1.md\`, \`81_FULL_TRANSCRIPT_STRONGER_VERSION_RESIDUAL_AUDIT_R1.md\`, \`83_FULL_TRANSCRIPT_CROSS_PLANE_LEAKAGE_AND_EMERGENT_TELEOLOGY_AUDIT_R1.md\`, and \`85_PERCEPTUAL_EPISTEMICS_FINAL_RESIDUE_AUDIT_R1.md\` at the same immutable source cut identified historically missed *combinations*: observer action as a lore-payment mechanic; a common ritual mistakenly treated as common meaning; completion driven exclusively by user gaze; user control actions covertly becoming fictional events; the visitor's helpful insight becoming retroactive destiny; learned attention models trained on their own curated exposure; passive ambient observation turning into omniscience; and knowledge turning into a neat personality simplification. These are now considered together as a **single UX corruption pattern**: the platform mistakes its power to *mediate* a relation for sovereignty to *produce, explain or own* the relation. The revised active HOME expresses each as a different refusal of that mistake, not a separate list of warnings. Original audit source status does not imply acceptance of new implementation owners, scientific calibration or full code proof.

**Final original-interaction source recheck:** SOROK \`01_PRODUCT_CHARTER.md\` (ethical horizon without compulsory conversion), \`08_INTERACTION_SYSTEM_SYNTHESIS_R1.md\`, \`09_INTERACTION_IDEATION_COMPLETENESS_AUDIT_R2.md\` and \`10_INTERACTION_IDEATION_COMPLETENESS_AUDIT_R3.md\` at historical cut \`a4211c3ec77e8269097601fe9a3dce2745395ac0\` confirm that first-person UX must distinguish **attempt from success, private intention from observed event, compound plan from realized action, and ATTEND from movement or manipulation of objects**. They further demand claim-provenance, no arbitrary observation quota, independent world time, counterfactual accessibility, and actor-limited rendering. These were recovered into movements 1, 4–5, 8, 13–15 and 21, not promoted as an additional artifact or an owner of constitutive psychology. Historical audit grades remain documentary, not an independently certified claim of complete source semantics.

**This section is part of the ONE HOME, not an additional source document.** What follows in the inherited conservation foundation is the exact preserved record of earlier theory sections, user-stated ideas and original SOROK source identities. It is not being promoted as an equal second theory; its function is to challenge each successive whole against claims it may have missed. The fully developed active articulation above must stand on its own. A source row's presence below is not by itself proof its meaning has been adequately fused above.


**This UX-first round recovered material directly from the original SOROK product and experiential courts at historical source cut \`a4211c3ec77e8269097601fe9a3dce2745395ac0\`:**

- **Courts 1–2, 5–8, D002 and original 26–29:** bounded real-human diegetic self, the four provisional CARRY/HOMOLOGIZE/BRACKET/USER-SELECT portal dimensions, medium-native cognitive work versus unverified declared ability, broad task as *entry into concrete affordances*, explicit delegation of low-salience procedure, user-controlled action granularity, downstream actor response, no morality inferred from input verbosity. **Fused into movements 1–5, 8, 13–14 and 21–22.** Original D002 29 corrects the too-early neutral autoexecution inference of original 28; this distinction is preserved.
- **Original 30–35, 43–48 and 52:** reciprocal curiosity, truthful invitation, minimal first primer, first-person situated portal versus outside messaging, scene ending through life, actual acquisition of phone routes, typed and nonmonotonic reachability, no automatic second contact, event-time and human-time distinction, pending scheduled meetings, no volitional user autopilot, read receipts and actor-local absence, optional strict sync without higher moral rank. **Fused into movements 1–2, 6–10, 13 and 21–22.** Concrete first-session scenes are prototypes, not fixed Nadia scripts.
- **Original 25–26, 45, 58–63, 80 and 87:** user-praxis reflection without diagnosis, unverifiable spectacle and honesty without lie policing, endogenous difficult people versus forbidden platform guilt optimization, governed rights to correct/export/delete, scene gestalt and selective attention, user-specific recognition without personalized world facts, actor testimony not a canon endpoint, full phenomenological anti-clue salience and controlled disclosure of the attention model. **Fused into movements 4–5, 8, 10, 12, 14–15 and 21.** These remain product-theory claims, not evidence of completed legal or scientific receiving.
- **Original 33–34, 44, 57 and 69:** person-to-person distribution, one deliberate human designation, credential commitment distinguished from recipient conversion, human-carried invitation, absence of referral prestige or urgency, distinct persona introduction and Roll, shared pre-contact roots with private branch histories, and the unresolved **relational warrant** question. **Fused into movements 10–12, 17 and 21.** Historical invitation constitution and warrant hypotheses retain their explicit provisionally adopted/open statuses; this HOME does not turn contested warrant into an invisible morality test.
- **Original 19–24, 38–41, 51 and 53–56:** material peer embedding, historically constrained specific interaction, third-party access controllers, objective external anchors versus synthetic local world, actor-owned disclosure, independent life and ordinary causal consequences. **Fused into movements 2–10, 13–15 and 19–22.** Person-psychological formation and its current ownership remain SORACLE-CO's separate theoretical jurisdiction, while UX incorporates the encounter-level demands.

This list identifies original-source draws and their *claimed* reconstitution in the active HOME. It does not convert historical provisional court statuses into accepted mandates or prove semantic exhaustion of all the source corpus.

The strongest source introductions in this round, with their status and reconstitution:

- **Original SOROK D007 kernel derivation and hostile tests** (immutable historical source cut a4211c3...): plural value centers, material relational formation, partial access, irreversible consequence, constrained non-exhaustion; the contradictions between love and self-erasure, commitment and possession, coherence and actuality, first-personhood and computational representation. **Reconstituted through the encounter kernel in movements 1, 8–10, 14–21 and 22**. The kernel remains provisional, not a newly accepted scientific law.
- **Original SOROK 69–73 theory**, same source cut: real-user agency without sovereignty; source-root social introduction and comparative forks; bounded historical slack and human contradiction; meaning strata; self-myth from one age reinterpreted at another; reality-metabolism, generous or tyrannical self-retcon and Horizon. **Reconstituted across movements 7–9, 11, 15, 20–22**. The named framework-response operators remain phenomenological alternatives, not mandatory enum definitions.

Specific alpha entitlements, privacy implementations and source owners remain unsettled.

A separate independent source-to-prose semantic proof has not yet been executed for every original identity.
